{"forms":[{"form":"centipede games","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An extensive form game in which two players take turns choosing either to take a slightly larger share of an increasing pot, or to pass the pot to the other player. The payoffs are arranged so that if one passes the pot to one's opponent and the opponent takes the pot on the next round, one receives slightly less than if one had taken the pot on this round, but after an additional switch the potential payoff will be higher. Therefore, although at each round a player has an incentive to take the pot, it would be better for them to wait."],"topics":["computing","engineering","game-theory","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":8918820,"upstream_offset":21611686399,"upstream_record_sha256":"0729180fb432957aa4253a0c545a5a020a9034839cfc3ac1df90631f546e0241","word":"centipede game"}

{"forms":[{"form":"dreams","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"dreame","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Last night I had a vividly scary dream about an immortal snail that chased me around and could kill me if it touched me.","type":"example"},{"text":"Crazy things like that only happen in dreams.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Imaginary events seen in the mind while sleeping."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"sweven"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"This year I fulfilled a lifelong dream of visiting the South Pacific.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A hope or wish."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"examples":[{"text":"live in a dream","type":"example"},{"text":"wake up from a dream","type":"example"},{"text":"impossible dream","type":"example"},{"text":"a dream of bliss","type":"example"},{"text":"the dream of his youth","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A visionary scheme; a wild conceit; an idle fancy."],"synonyms":[{"word":"vision"}]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/dɹiːm/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[dɹ̝ʷɪi̯m]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[d̠ɹ̠˔ʷɪi̯m]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/dɹim/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":30482,"upstream_offset":205248724,"upstream_record_sha256":"26c88f4788dae95bf6c669e857712855e625b87b7b5fd25d9622d5e4fab0ad12","word":"dream"}
{"forms":[{"form":"dreams","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"dreaming","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"dreamed","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"dreamed","tags":["past"]},{"form":"dreamt","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"dreamt","tags":["past"]},{"form":"dreame","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Although people primarily dream during the REM phase of sleeping, they can dream during non-REM sleep as well.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To see imaginary events in one's mind while sleeping."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Lucy dreams of becoming a scientist when she grows up.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To hope, to wish."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Stop dreaming and get back to work.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To daydream."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I dreamed a vivid dream last night.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To envision as an imaginary experience (usually when asleep)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I wouldn't dream of snubbing you in public.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To consider the possibility (of)."],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/dɹiːm/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[dɹ̝ʷɪi̯m]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[d̠ɹ̠˔ʷɪi̯m]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/dɹim/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":30483,"upstream_offset":205339381,"upstream_record_sha256":"7b8a4b19c79e0fcc347f9ba061807bb85c888781b9170c31a21819cd7fbfdd8d","word":"dream"}

{"forms":[{"form":"winters","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"Winter","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Traditionally the fourth of the four seasons, typically regarded as spanning either the period between the winter solstice to the spring equinox, or the months of December, January, and February in temperate and polar regions of the Northern Hemisphere and the months of June, July, and August in the Southern Hemisphere.
